# The Darknet District — current AI handoff

**Start here.** This file is the one current handoff. Read the checklist and the sections through "Open questions" before any other handoff document. The dated diary under [Historical log](#historical-log) is kept so older notes are not lost. Entries in that log contradict each other. They are snapshots, not instructions. The owner's reference pictures from 12 Nov 2026 are written out in [`VISUAL_REFERENCES.md`](VISUAL_REFERENCES.md) so later work can use them without the files. The images are not in the repo yet ([`references/README.md`](references/README.md)).

**Checked against:** `origin/main` `4fc1a70` (12 Nov 2026). That commit is the merge of #240 (Tropical Escape dolphins) on top of `7939256` (NULL nightclub, #238). Re-fetch GitHub before trusting pull-request numbers or deploy claims below.

### 21 Nov 2026 (newest): IRIS'S AMBIENT COMMS LINES, AND IRIS FILLS THE BR-04 GAP (branch `claude/iris-ambient`, build `2026-11-21-iris-ambient-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

- **The lines:** the owner's 13 approved lines (21 Nov 2026; `VOID_RUNNER_HANDOFF.md` §11), recorded with ElevenLabs eleven_v4 in Iris's voice "Kristen" (`CvD6hF1BJzAFN428j1cO`), dry: `void-runner/assets/audio/voice/extra/iris-bark-*.ogg` (24 kHz mono vorbis), extra manifest entries (speaker `iris`, exact text, source `iris-ambient.js`). The manifest was rewritten by `node tools/audio/zen-import.cjs` (it keeps Zen's entries last; `--check` passes).
- **Rules (pure, unit-tested):** `void-runner/iris-ambient.js` (`VoidIrisAmbientRules`): the lines verbatim and ONE tunable table `TUNING`: global cooldown 210 s, the same line again 25 min, quiet lines (jokes, store) 7 min apart and only after 60 s of calm (no hack, sneak, combat, call, conversation, other voice), hack lines only on hacks of 8 s or more, once, between 42% and 72% of the hack, a sneak line once per visit to the sneak area while a guard within 8 m has not seen the player, the spotted line 4 s after SPOTTED (given up after 30 s), a wait line 25 s after a game-time wait step starts, the store line not in the first 10 min and at most once per session (memory only), jokes only while a mission is in progress. Never while busy (nothing queued), never in combat; the quiet lines never in a hostile zone or a sneak area; only once Iris is a contact (`story.flags.metIris` or Mission 001 completed).
- **The BR-04 gap (the owner, same day):** Mission 009 completed and Mission 010 not yet acquired: the wait line about 25 s after (`gapFirst`), then a joke or the store line (if not yet played this session) about every 70 s (`gapCooldown`, after 10 s of calm), no line twice; it stops the moment Mission 010 arrives. **No** "Wait for BR-04" objective was added (the owner chose Iris instead).
- **Game wiring:** `void-runner/iris-ambient-game.js` (loaded after `dev-test.js`, before `bootstrap.js`): polls twice a second. Hack: `VoidApartments.hacking` (all access-point hacks go through `apartment-integration.js run`). Sneak: `VoidMissionMechanicsGame.now()` unseen steps inside their location, `VoidZoneRun.enemies`, the seen meter. SPOTTED: one hook in `mission-mechanics-game.js seenCheck` (`VoidIrisAmbient.spotted()`). Busy: any mode but walking, a conversation (`speech`), a notice on screen, a Comms call or banner, the V1 open, a camera feed (BR-04 / the ritual), another voice clip or browser speech, the character creator, a draft play-test (`VoidDevTest.active`), a shared VR session. Shown as `Iris (Comms): <line>` in the notice line; the clip plays through `VoidAudio.speak('iris', text)` (voice off: the words only).
- **Switches:** Settings > **IRIS CHATTER: ON/OFF** (this browser, localStorage `void-runner-iris-chatter`). Developer Tools > Test & Jump To > **Iris ambient lines**: a checkbox (this session), the numbers, each line with when it last played, and PREVIEW buttons per kind. Nothing is saved with the campaign; no save format change.
- **Tests:** `tests/iris-ambient.test.cjs` (texts verbatim, every clip present with the exact text, hack / sneak / spotted / wait / quiet rules, cooldowns, store once per session, busy, voice off, the BR-04 gap). Browser: `tests/iris-ambient-browser.py` (Chromium, software GL): a 10 s hack at a stand-in access point, the hack line at 45% ("Iris (Comms): Just a little longer."), its clip played, no page errors. Admin inventory regenerated.
- **Not verified:** a real sneak / SPOTTED / BR-04 gap in a played mission in the browser (rules unit-tested only), how the lines sound over the live android effect in a real session, real GPU / ROG Ally.

### 21 Nov 2026: ROOK'S CARGO SIDE JOBS ARE PROCEDURAL (branch `claude/rook-procedural-cargo`, build `2026-11-21-rook-cargo-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

- The owner: "For Rook after this make cargo missions Procedural". After Mission 012 (story flag `rook-first-delivery-done`) Rook's District board is generated by the new pure module `void-runner/rook-cargo-gen.js` (loaded after `rook-cargo-jobs.js`, before `campaign.js`). One job per kind in `rook-cargo-jobs.js districtBoard` (routine, long-haul, high-value, dangerous lane: the kinds, labels, pay-at and danger are unchanged); each gets a seeded destination (only known places in known systems whose warp marker is known; never the `never` list or a system Admin's delivery holds; far kinds leave Erebus; quiet jobs prefer local lanes, dangerous ones far lanes; unserved systems likelier), goods from a generic list (`rook-cargo-jobs.js procedural`: general freight, medical supplies, machine parts, data cores, perishable food, water filters, coolant cells, spare electronics, building materials), a container count and weight (weight sets hold spaces, never more than the ship holds), a flavour title ("Priority haul") and pay = the old run-number formula + kind bonus, times distance (landing +10%, another system +30%, faction system +15%), +15% per extra hold space, +/-10% variety, rounded to 10 CR.
- Determinism: seeded by `story.flags.rookSeed` (random in `startDistrictCampaign`; a save with the board open but no seed gets one derived from the save on load) and the run number, so a reload shows the same board and every finished run brings a new one. No time limits / fragile / no-scan: no existing mechanics for them. No dev reroll button (Dev Tools has no Rook hooks).
- An accepted job stores its terms in `story.flags.rookTerms` (`units|credits|kg|cargo|title`, <=200 chars), cleared on delivery or hand-back. A job accepted on the older board (no `rookTerms`) keeps its older terms. The older non-District board (`rookBoard` outside a District campaign) is unchanged. Rook's two lines (`lines.coordinates`, `lines.board`) are unchanged.
- Server: no change (both flags are ordinary story flags `clean_save()` already accepts; `tests/test_story_save.py` checks it); no Render redeploy needed. `save-manifest.json` unchanged (no new ids).
- Board UI: each job shows kind, a flavour title, cargo, destination, weight, hold spaces and pay. Fixed a layout bug in the chat panel: the sticky choice buttons covered the job list (`play-ui.css`: the buttons follow the list when a Rook board is shown; the panel scrolls).
- Tests: `tests/rook-cargo-gen.test.cjs`; `flight-map-coords` pay/units assertion updated. Known quirk (pre-existing, same on `main`): a reload wakes the player in NeoChinatown, so a job to NeoChinatown (`earth`) taken before a reload is completed and paid by the next flight (which goes to Meridian). Not changed here.
### 21 Nov 2026 (newest): SITTING DOWN AND STANDING UP AT THE FLOOR SEATS (branch `claude/floor-transitions`, built on `claude/seat-poses`, build `2026-11-21-floor-transitions-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner asked for a proper standing-up animation for the floor poses (and a matching sit-down was promised). Before: a cross-fade into the pose while the figure was eased up to the seat's height.

- **The clips (ten per figure, one-shot, posed by code in `tools/animation-pipeline/make_floor_poses.py` `TRANS`; no Blender, no download, no AI or outside service):** `floor-kneel-down` (knees bend forward, right knee down onto the cushion, then the left, toes tucked, upright, sit back on the heels), `floor-cross-down` / `floor-knees-down` (squat with the arms forward, a hand back onto the cushion, down, then the shins cross / the arms go round the shins), and for the raised seats `floor-cross-down-hi` / `floor-knees-down-hi` (standing on the floor in front of a cot or alcove bench: sit on the edge, lean back on the hands, legs up, push back). Each `-up` is its `-down` reversed (stand-up with the hand push). 1.4 to 2 s. Each `-down` starts exactly on the figure's idle frame 0 and ends exactly on the loop's first frame; `-up` the reverse (checked numerically for every figure; fingers are not animated in them, they keep the previous clip's curl).
- **Who:** the same 68 figures that have the floor poses (`crowd.js FLOOR_MOVES`; character specs and `clips.json` list them, `source: pose`). Not the companions, licensed files, the Blackrock robe, the cat or Zen Zephyr (untouched). About +140 KB per figure file (+9 MB in the crowd folder).
- **Runtime (`ncz-street-pair.js` `FLOOR_MOVE`, `floorFx`, modes `floor-down` / `floor-up`; used by Reverie, Lace and the made characters via `npc-made.js`):** root motion shared with the generator: she stands at S (d metres in front of the loop's origin: kneel -0.08, cross 0.18, knees 0.32; -hi 0.74 / 0.84), and over the clip her origin moves to the loop's origin and her lift goes h - hb*(1-r) (hb 0 on cushions, 0.45 on cots/benches), r = smoothstep, so the feet stay planted and nothing floats or sinks. On a cushion she steps up onto it first (lift rises at most 0.6 m/s over the last 0.3 m; the dais cushions from the aisle); off a dais / cot / bench she walks back to the stand point stepping down. The routine waits the clip's length, then the loop; leaving: the `-up`, then walking. The runtimes pass `has` (the figure's clip list) and play the clip with `playOnce(clip, after)`. A figure without the clips: the old cross-fade, unchanged. The stool / chair path unchanged. The lift easing now runs at the top of `routineStep` (also while a plan walks her).
- **Not choosable:** `npc-gestures.js NO` filters `floor-*-down/-up`; the Mission Editor's cast list and the server's `CAST_CLIPS` do not have them. Clip index and admin inventory regenerated (no server rule changed: no Render redeploy needed for this).
- **Tests:** `tests/floor-transitions.test.cjs` (clips on every figure, ends equal idle / loop frames numerically with meshopt decoding, lengths and root-motion numbers equal between Python and JS, the routine's walk / down / loop / up / walk at a cushion, a dais cushion, two cots and a bench without position or lift jumps, the fallback and the stool unchanged); `seat-poses.test.cjs` no longer pins BUILD. Browser check `tests/floor-transitions-browser.py` (Python Playwright, software GL): nine figures frozen half-way down, seated, half-way up, early down and late up at the tea room cushions, the dais, a cot and an alcove bench; feet / hips measured; looked at.
- **Not verified:** a real GPU / ROG Ally; VR; a placed NPC walking the whole sequence live in the browser (the routine is unit-tested; the browser check freezes clips where the runtime places them); Babylon's 0.06 per-frame cross-fade into the loop starts the loop at the figure's random phase (a small blend, not seen frozen); the stepping up onto a dais cushion is an eased lift, not a step animation.

Browser (Python Playwright, bundled Chromium, software GL): an associate line and Fish's first call line load (200) and play, Fish's with the English 0.75 s over it. **Not verified:** a real GPU / ROG Ally, VR, the whole Mission 004 / 007 / 010 played through by hand.
### 20 Nov 2026 (newest): PLANET DESCENTS AND TAKE-OFFS; NO DEAD ENDS IN SPACE (branch `claude/planet-landing`, build `2026-11-20-planet-landing-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner: "I've also never flown from a space station to a planet yet ... it should probably have one so players don't get stuck out in space and can't return to Servo or land on other planets. it would be cool if the landing zones coming in matched what each area looked like ie: NeoChinaTown even if things had to be estimated or faked".

- **Audit (before this build):** a flight to a planet location (NeoChinatown `earth`, Vantor `mars`, The Nexus, the Natural Order homeworld, Vesper) ended with the same automatic docking as a station: the walkable place simply loaded (`world-integration.js arrive` -> `dock`; homeworlds through `homeworld-scenes.js`), with no descent; boarding on a planet started the flight with no take-off. The marker logic (`nav-coords.js`) has no dead end: from every place the map can reach, in route-only, known-places and all-locations modes, before and after a warp, a chain of markers leads home and every flight completes (now a unit test). **Two real dead ends were found and fixed:** (1) a District player **without a ship** anywhere but NeoChinatown (a draft play or test that puts them at a station) could never get back: BOARD said "You do not have a ship yet." / "Talk to the station contact before boarding." **Now** BOARD there takes a passenger shuttle home to the Port (`VoidLanding.shuttleHome`, the descent plays without the cockpit; flag `shuttleFrom`, a short string); (2) a flight landing at NeoChinatown could put the player back inside the District or in the last quarter they walked (flags `area` / `quarter`): `campaign.js complete` now clears both on a landing at `earth`, so a flight always sets down **at the Port**.
- **The descent (`void-runner/planet-landing.js`, `VoidLanding`; loaded after `flight-nav.js`):** about 22 s, cockpit view (the painted Kestrel cockpit, `cockpit-overlay.js`): orbit (the planet fills the canopy; night side and city lights for Servo), atmospheric entry (plasma glow, heat shimmer, shake), the cloud layer (rain and canopy drops on Servo, dust on Vantor / Vesper), the final approach over the landing area at its own time of day, the hover, turn and touchdown on the pad (gear thump, steam on Servo's wet pad, dust elsewhere). It starts when a flight ends at a planetside place; the walkable place loads underneath at the same time (dock() unchanged) and the view hands over when both are done. One small Babylon scene on the game's engine, disposed after each sequence; the game's update is held while it plays, its draw replaced.
- **The landing areas (estimates from the real data only):** NeoChinatown from `neochinatown.js` (every street rect, the footprints, the city box; plots cut like the walkable city's with its floor counts; the outer ring, the blocks out to 300 m, the megacity tower rings) using the walkable city's own night facade textures; the Port pad and Pad 01 markings with approach strobes, the gates, the District (brick, 13 m) with the alley's magenta / violet wash and haze, lamps along every street, lit shop-front strips, blade signs on the wide streets, the Outskirts' container homes (`ncz-containers.js` plan), the Corporate District's blocks (`corporate.js`) to the **west**, beyond the haze (set at x -1250 and heights x2.2 so they read: an estimate). The approach comes in from the east over the District and Main Street, lines up over the passage, turns south and sets down where the Kestrel stands (x 22, z -96, nose south). Vantor: the mars-terminal blocks as the access head at the surface, a lit shaft, vent stacks, sodium masts, rust desert and mesas at night. The Nexus: the planets.js buildings, the core hall and broken spire, pale ruined towers with a few cyan seams, overcast. The Natural Order homeworld: the barn, silos, workshop, farmhouse and stalls, field patchwork, farmsteads, solar arrays, windbreaks, daylight. Vesper: the workshop yard among desert ridges. A flight line clearance test keeps every NeoChinatown camera position 8 m above the roofs.
- **The take-off:** boarding on a planet (a launch from the ground in the open game; the old Vesper opening's own cockpit flow is untouched) plays about 14 s: lift-off with steam / dust and the gear coming up, the climb-out over the area, the cloud layer, space; then the existing flight with its departure phase. **Mission 012 and the beginner campaign:** their board / take off / jump / dock triggers poll `mode` and `flight.route`; the sequence never changes either (only holds the update), so they fire as before (board as soon as the flight is ready, take off when the route reaches `align` after the ascent). A held launch (refused, failed) ends the ascent at once so its message shows.
- **Skip and setting:** SKIP button, Space, Enter or Escape (jumps to the touchdown; if the place is still loading, "PORT SYSTEMS · CONNECTING" holds until it is ready). Settings > Flight > **Play landing sequences** On / Off (default On; this browser only, localStorage `void-landing-sequences`). Esc in flight pauses it with the game menu; leaving to the main menu ends it.
- **Sound:** `flight-sfx.js` gained `VoidFlightSfx.play(name)` and synthesized `arrive`, `entry`, `wind`, `thrusters`, `gear`, `touchdown`, `steam`, `liftoff` (the effects channel; volume and mute apply).
- **Saves:** nothing new to migrate. `story.flags.shuttleFrom` (a short string) only on a shuttle ride; `area` / `quarter` are cleared on a landing at NeoChinatown. No server change; `save-manifest.json` unchanged.
- **Tests:** `tests/planet-landing.test.cjs` (always a way home in every mode and after a warp, every planet location landable with a walkable scene and BOARD, the Port landing, the shuttle, timelines, camera lines end on the pad facing the way the ship stands, the NeoChinatown plan from the real layout and roof clearance, the wiring); `tests/planet-landing-browser.py` (Python Playwright, software GL: Meridian -> Tessaly Belt -> NeoChinatown descent and Port landing, take-off and Vantor landing, the other descents, SKIP, the setting, the shuttle).
- **Not verified:** a real GPU / ROG Ally frame rate (software GL only; the city is about 1,300 instanced boxes in ~60 draw groups, Low drops the outer towers and glow); VR; the whole Mission 012 tutorial played through the take-off in a browser (its triggers were checked in code and the route phases in the browser test); sound by ear (a headless browser cannot listen).
### 20 Nov 2026 (newest): NEVER STUCK IN AN INTERIOR; ONE DOOR TIMING; THE NEXT PLACE SHOWN IN EVERY DOORWAY (branch `claude/apt-stuck`, build `2026-11-20-apt-stuck-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner: "look for reasons you might get stuck in a housing room and can't move once you enter. maybe stuck in the couch or something else"; then: check the door animations (speed, how long they stay open, the fade starting too early); then: every housing door is "really a short loading screen": slide open and show part of the next area in the doorway, both directions, everywhere (faked is fine).

- **The trap found:** the three city apartments (rundown, normal, luxury) put the player at local [0, -3.55], inside BOTH the couch's and the low table's collision (they stand 0.35 m and 0.3 m from that point; the player is 0.38 m round). The walk refuses any move whose end point overlaps a solid, so every key was refused: stuck until E on LEAVE THE APARTMENT. Mission 005 (Reyes's luxury apartment 105) hit it. Fixed: `apartments.js APT.spawn` [-0.3, -3.85] (between the entry door and the couch / table). Also: the underground tea room's back door (`tea-under.n1`) put an arriving player 1.25 m in, inside the partition and the tea station; a module cluster entered with no door named fell back to the location's far-off middle (inside a desk / rack / tunnel wall). Fixed in `modules.js layout()`: the arrival point moves along the line in from the door, then to the nearest free spot (`VoidExplorer.free`); a cluster without a named door uses its first door.
- **Checked and fine:** both hallways (street door, every slot's door front, the fire-escape window), both container apartments, every module type through every door, elevator and stairwell arrivals, every template, the story missions' locations, the tunnels' checkpoints and stair landings, the street doors' and fire escapes' outside points; NPCs have no collision; items placed by missions add no collision; the computer / screen / lights / vending / closet / safe / hack hand input straight back (each calls resumeWalking; the hack runs while walking).
- **The safety net (`exploration-controller.js`):** `depth()` lets a player who overlaps a solid walk OUT of it (never further in, never through a wall from outside); `free()` finds the nearest standable spot; `watch()` is the quiet unstick: a movement key held 2 s with no movement and no direction free (5 cm probes) puts him on the nearest free spot (walking into a wall never triggers it). `babylon-integration.js enterWalking` moves an arrival point that is inside a solid in apartments / modules / the tunnels (never the Sleeping Pod's wake view, which is inside the pod row on purpose). Module doors stay open while the player is within 2.2 m (they cannot close on him in the doorway).
- **Doors (one timing, `apartments.js DOOR`):** open 0.6 s eased (was 0.5 s), held 1.5 s after nobody is near, the fade waits 0.62 s from E (was a fixed 450 ms / 350 ms, the door 90 % open), then a 0.18 s fade in, the place swapped behind it with the game's silent load (was the full-screen "Preparing ..." card), a 0.32 s fade out. Module doors open from 2.2 m (was 1.8: a run met the closed leaves). The tunnels' stair door now slides open (it never moved: a fade only).
- **Doorway views (`void-runner/door-views.js`, pictures `void-runner/art/door-views/*.webp`, 256 x 512, baked by `tools/door-views/bake.cjs` from the real interiors):** street door: its hallway (or the module location's first room); hallway street door: the street of that quarter at night; hallway door: the apartment; apartment door: the hallway; module exits: the street / the linked room; tunnels' stair door: the checkpoint; checkpoint door: the stair landing. A dark jamb reveal and an unlit picture per door, one material per picture per place, nothing rendered live. Elevators open onto the real lobby already (same cluster), so they need no picture. Fire-escape windows unchanged.
- **Tests:** `tests/interior-spawns.test.cjs` (every arrival point: no overlap, 1.5 m of room, the way out reachable by flood fill with the walk's own collision; the walk-out and the unstick), `tests/door-views.test.cjs` (timing, every picture baked and small, every door kind wired). Re-bake the pictures after changing an interior's look: `node tests/serve-modern.cjs`, then `node tools/door-views/bake.cjs` (ONLY=key,key).
- **Not verified:** real hardware; the pictures' match to the arrival view is approximate (a short fade covers it); the corporate maintenance door shows the Avenue's street.

### 21 Nov 2026 (newest): THE MISSION EDITOR NO LONGER CLOSES BY ITSELF OR POPS UP DURING DRAFT PLAY (branch `claude/editor-timeout`, build `2026-11-21-editor-timeout-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner: "sometimes the mission editor times out and goes to the previous page. also while trying to play through a test mission the editor pops up". How-to and code map: [`MISSION_EDITOR.md`](MISSION_EDITOR.md), "Fix (21 Nov 2026): the editor stays open".

- **Cause of both (reproduced in a browser on the old code):** `account.js` re-checks the account every 4 minutes and when the browser tab gets focus back; each check called `VoidDevTools.identity()` again, which hid the Developer tools window (the game or main menu behind it showed: "the previous page") and, while the `mission-editor-return` note was waiting (written by PLAY THIS DRAFT for LEAVE DRAFT), reopened the Mission Editor over the draft being played. Second cause of the first symptom: a new BUILD deployed while the editor was open over the main menu reloaded the page by itself (`update-ready.js` counted the main menu as safe).
- **Now:** a re-check of the same account changes nothing (`editor-session.js identity`: swap / hold / expired / fresh / close); the editor opens by itself only on the first confirmation after a page load (LEAVE DRAFT's reload), never during draft play; signed out with the editor or a draft open: it stays open with a small "Sign-in expired … SIGN IN" bar (`VoidAccount.reconfirm`: forced token refresh, Google popup only when signed out); any 401 is retried once with a freshly refreshed token (`account.js api`); the editor's server copy retries by itself with a growing wait (3, 6, 12, 24, 45, 60 s) and says "Server waking up / retrying…" (the draft is in localStorage all along; a field being typed in is committed on `pagehide`); a new build never reloads once the Developer tools were opened on the page or during a draft: an "Update ready … APPLY UPDATE" bar does it on click; Escape in a text box only leaves the box; the DEVELOPER TOOLS button and every test/draft start drop keyboard focus (Space / Enter in the game cannot press them). Draft play: a failed step (lost follow, SPOTTED, taken down with "fail") shows a small banner note "Draft: step failed — … RETRY it in the game, or press LEAVE DRAFT · BACK TO EDITOR"; the game's own retry rules are unchanged.
- **Code:** new `void-runner/editor-session.js` (pure, loaded after `update-ready.js`), `dev-tools.js` (identity, bar, Escape, focus, pagehide, `used`), `account.js` (uid passed, 401 retry, `reconfirm`), `update-ready.js` + `cache.js` (`editor` waits, bar), `mission-editor.js` (`trouble` retries, banner label), `dev-test.js` (`stepNote`, blur), one-line hooks in `live-content.js`, `mission-mechanics-game.js`, `zone-integration.js`; `dev-tools.css`. No server or save change.
- **Tests:** `tests/editor-timeout.test.cjs` (every rule and the wiring); `tests/editor-timeout-browser.cjs` (bundled Chromium, software GL: account check while typing, Escape, sign-out bar, two server timeouts then saved, update wait + bar, draft play with an account check and Space / E / F / V / Escape / ` / Enter, the step-failed note, BACK TO EDITOR; `EXPECT_OLD=1` lists what the old code got wrong: 17 of 22 checks failed there).
- **Limits:** the SIGN IN bar's Google popup is the only sign-in it offers when fully signed out (handle / email-link accounts: open Pilot account after closing the editor); after the editor was opened, a waiting update applies only on APPLY UPDATE or a manual reload (also on the main menu).
### 20 Nov 2026 (newest): DISTANCES SHOWN IN FEET (branch `claude/feet-units`, build `2026-11-20-feet-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner: "can you change the distances to feet instead of meters".

- **Display only.** The game, physics, saves, mission data, the server and its checks keep **metres**. No save or mission changes; no server change (no Render redeploy needed).
- **One helper: `void-runner/units.js` (`VoidUnits`, pure, loaded first in `void-runner.html`).** `fmtDist(m)`: whole feet with comma grouping ("144 ft", "4,921 ft"), from one mile "1.2 mi" (whole miles from 10). `ft(m)` / `m(feet, oldMetres, {min, max, whole})` / `ftRange(minM, maxM)` for editor boxes; `text(s)` rewrites "1 to 30 metres", "15 m" in a message into feet (coordinates, "m/s" and numberless wording are left alone). Use it for any new distance shown to people.
- **Players see feet:** the mission arrow (`world-integration.js`), the walking caption, the yellow photo TARGET marker (`mission-guide.js`), world markers (`world-markers.js`), the V1 map (scale bar in feet, place distance, the waypoint chip "… FT", taxi distance), the street taxi messages and HUD card (`road-taxi.js`), nearby players in the V1 (`personal-device.js`), photo details in the gallery, Skirmish's NEAREST, Mission 004's "stay within about twenty feet" objective.
- **The owner sees feet:** Mission Editor (walked-near distance, the wait step's "stays within", escort "waits when … behind" / "lost beyond", summaries, help, every problem list and the server's check / publish answers), the Blocks view, the Create-a-mission wizard and home page, the mechanics fields (within / lost beyond / too close), the Blackrock scene editor (radius, gap), NPC import height, tunnel module names, NPC-area and placed-item messages. A box nobody changed stores the exact metres; a changed one stores feet / 3.28084 to 3 decimals (whole metres where the stored form must be whole). Details: `MISSION_EDITOR.md` "For Claude and Codex".
- **Left in metres (on purpose):** map coordinates (x / z readouts and boxes in the NPC map, mechanics, zones editor), speeds (m/s), Developer Tools > Balance numbers (detection range, flee radius, chase speed: raw tuning values), the AI-prompt text in `npc-behaviour.js`, code comments. **Flight** keeps its game unit "u" (target range, RNG, TGT); nothing in flight showed metres.
- **Tests:** `tests/feet-units.test.cjs` (formatting, rounding, miles, round trips, the built-in missions unchanged, server messages, wiring); `district-people`, `places-names` updated; browser scripts `editor-givers-browser.cjs`, `npc-escort-browser.cjs`, `district-map-browser.cjs` follow the feet boxes. Checked in Chromium (software GL): the V1 map, the arrow / waypoint chip, the Mission Editor walk-near box (16 ft default; 20 ft stores 6.096 m; saving again keeps it).

### 20 Nov 2026 (newest): FOLLOW / LEAD STEPS CROSS GATES, THE STAIRS TO THE TUNNELS AND THE DISTRICT DOOR, WALKING OR IN A TAXI (branch `claude/escort-across`, build `2026-11-20-escort-across-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner: a follow / lead step did not carry the person through a city gate, or up / down the stairwells between the street and the tunnels: "I need this fixed. whether it's walking or in a taxi". How-to and code map: [`MISSION_EDITOR.md`](MISSION_EDITOR.md), "Twenty-seventh pass".

- **Now:** a "Follow them" / "They follow you" step starts where she is placed but may end in any area she can walk to (The Avenue, Old Town, Far South, The Outskirts, The Port, The Underground, the District inside). Leading, she walks to the next gate lane / stairwell / District door and waits outside it; when the player goes through she comes through ahead of them and leads on. Followed, she comes through behind them. Lost / wait settings apply at the crossing (stay and the arrow leads back to her, or the mission fails). Where she may not go (Milked and the adult places for Reverie / Lace / non-adult made characters, the Corporate District and the Temple, the cat inside or underground) she stops at the boundary with a message and the editor shows the problem. In any follow / lead step she rides along in the player's taxi (seated), through gate bays into other quarters, and gets out with the player; cab steps may go to any city stop. Made characters ride too (seated if their model has a sit clip, else hidden behind the glass). The arrow: on her while she leads; to the destination (any area) while she follows the player; to her position when she is in another area.
- **Code:** `npc-escort.js` (ACROSS section: links, routes, across, rideAlong; stops / problems use `reach`), new `npc-escort-across.js` (wraps `enterWalking`, loaded after `ncz-gate-transit.js`), `live-content.js` (plan area / leg / carry), `ncz-street-pair.js`, `npc-made.js`, `mission-guide.js` + one hook in `world-integration.js`, `mission-editor.js` (wording, the spot map's area list unlocked), `editor-home.js` / `mission-givers.js` wording. **Server Python changed:** `void_runner_live_content.py` (`_reach`: a spot or place door in another reachable area passes; cab stop text) and the regenerated admin inventory (`npcEscort.npcs[id].reach`, wider `stopsByArea`). No save shape change.
- **Tests:** `tests/escort-across.test.cjs` (links, routes, gate / stairs up and down / District door, across with / stay / fail / refused, taxi ride-along across a gate, cat and adult rules, wiring); updated npc-escort, npc-areas, follow-into, follow-giver, underground (BUILD) tests and the Python tests. Browser (Python Playwright, bundled Chromium, software GL, scratchpad script): see the session report for the three runs.
- **Limits:** a person left in another area does not walk on by herself (she waits where she was); the street pair's figure is rebuilt in the new area after the exchange, so she appears a moment after the scan clears; made characters' seated pose uses Reverie's seat height; the District door is routed only from Old Town; Milked is never a route for anyone (adult made characters may still follow the player in if the area rules allow).

### 20 Nov 2026 (newest): FLIGHT MAP WARP MARKERS (ROUTE ONLY / ALL LOCATIONS) AND ROOK'S CARGO SIDE MISSIONS (branch `claude/flight-map-coords`, build `2026-11-20-flight-map-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner: "For now with the mission to Rook I just want to keep it simple and only show the warp markers that are needed to get to Meridian Station. But once you deliver to Rook I want him to offer you cargo missions to other places as side missions. He will tell you that he's just sent you coordinates of locations throughout the world and it updates your flying Map App with the locations automatically. ... I want the Map App while flying to show all of the warp markers to every location within the system you're flying in as well as warp markers to the other systems you can get to from the system you're in."

- **Three marker modes (`void-runner/nav-coords.js`, `VoidNav`, pure + browser; loaded right after `mission-log.js`, before `campaign.js`).** `world-integration.js flightRoutes()` and the in-flight Map app (`flight-nav.js entries / markerFor / view`) show `VoidNav.markers(state, active missions)`, always a subset of `universe.js destinations()` (so every marker can be flown to; a marker keeps its angle whatever the set):
  - **ALL LOCATIONS** (`story.flags.navCoords === true`): every location of the system you fly in, plus the warp marker (hub) of every other system you know. The coordinates make every Erebus and Tessaly place known; a faction system stays as its own story opened it (homeworlds stay closed).
  - **ROUTE ONLY** (no coordinates, an active mission somewhere else): only `nextHop` toward each active mission's destination (e.g. from NeoChinatown just the Meridian warp marker), plus the way home to NeoChinatown (The Port). The Map app says "ROUTE ONLY".
  - **KNOWN PLACES** (no coordinates, no mission elsewhere): the discovered places, as before. Nobody is ever left with zero markers (an empty route falls back to these).
  - The blue NAV square and yellow MISSION squares (flight-nav.js) are unchanged; they appear on whichever markers are shown.
- **Saves.** `navCoords` is a plain boolean in `story.flags` (cloud saves already accept it; no server change for it). `campaign.js awardStarterShip` (the Mission Editor's "Give the player a ship") sets `navCoords=false` when unset, in a District campaign: **a new Kestrel pilot starts in route-only mode**. `campaign.js restore()` calls `VoidNav.migrate`: a save with `navCoords` unset that owns a ship, flies freely (`universe.freeTravel`) and has visited a place other than NeoChinatown/Vesper, or knows a faction-system place, or is an older open-pool campaign past the opening, gets the coordinates (`navCoords=true`, every Erebus/Tessaly place known): **nobody who flies freely today loses a marker**. An explicit `false` is never upgraded.
- **THE INTERFACE (for the Awakening / First Cargo Delivery tree, branch `claude/awakening-tree`):**
  - `VoidNav.grantCoordinates(reason)` (browser): sets `navCoords`, discovers every Erebus and Tessaly location, saves, refreshes the Map app if open, shows the V1 banner "NAV DATA RECEIVED · every Erebus and Tessaly System location added to your Map". Idempotent (returns true only when something changed). Pure version: `VoidNav.grant(s, reason)` (Node: `require('./nav-coords.js')`). A grant made any other way (the editor unlock) gets the same banner within a second (the flag is watched; loading a save shows nothing).
  - Mission Editor step effect: unlock kind **`nav-coords`** ("Unlock all nav coordinates") in "What becomes available", "at step X" or "when the mission is done" (`mission-givers.js UNLOCK_KINDS`, `mission-content.js UNLOCKS / unlockNow`, the editor's unlock row; the server reads `unlockKinds` from `void_runner_admin_inventory.json`, regenerated).
  - **`story.flags['rook-first-delivery-done'] = true`**: set when the first delivery to Rook is done: Mission 012 (First Cargo Delivery, #457) sets it with its "Shared game flag" unlock (`contract-flag`, id `rook-first-delivery-done`). This one flag is the only switch (an `mf_` story flag does not open the board). This opens Rook's side missions. The tree should not call `grantCoordinates` itself: Rook does on the next talk.
- **Rook's side missions (reuse the existing cargo network, `campaign.js` rook-run).** In a District campaign (closed mission pool) Rook's board was shut (`Missions.closed`); it now opens with the flag (`C.rookOpen`). Talking to Rook (when no tree step waits on him): the first time, his coordinates line (PROPOSED) and `grantCoordinates('rook')` (`story.flags.rookNavSent`), then his board (`C.districtBoard`): one job of each kind in `districtBoard` (routine, long-haul, high-value, dangerous lane), each to a different reachable place (known place, known system, not held by Admin's faction delivery; never Vesper, a homeworld or Meridian), deterministic per run number (a reload never rerolls it, every finished run moves it on). Taking a job: contract `rook-run`, a Mission Log entry `rook-run-N` of type **side** ("SIDE" in the log), tracked, the yellow marker/route to it; pay on delivery or by Rook at Meridian, exactly once (the existing rules and tests). Before the flag, Rook says his existing line "Deliver the cargo. Then come back for more work." (before this a District player talking to Rook with a ship hit an empty board and a script error).
  - **The job list is data the owner can edit: `void-runner/rook-cargo-jobs.js`** (kinds, labels, cargo, hold space, pay formula, bonus, danger, which kinds are on the District board, places never used, Rook's two lines). The older board (open-pool saves) reads the same table, unchanged values.
  - **Rook's lines, APPROVED by the owner 21 Nov 2026** (no clip yet; text only until recorded): coordinates: "Good run. I just sent you coordinates for places all over. Stations, planets. Your Map app already has them. Means I can send you just about anywhere." ("landing pads" and "anywhere" corrected to what the game does, branch `claude/kestrel-lines`). Board: "Got freight going all over. Take a job, the pay is on the board."
  - Save rules: no new contract or mission ids (`rook-run` is already in `save-manifest.json`; `rook-run-N` records pass). `void_runner_api.py clean_save` (closed pool) now keeps a tracked `rook-run-N` when the flag (or its `mf_` form) is set, as `campaign.js retireMissions` and `mission-content.js migrate` do.
- **Tests:** `tests/flight-map-coords.test.cjs` (route-only to Meridian, known fallback, full mode, idempotent grant, migration, the editor unlock and the server list, the board only after the flag, reachable places only, a side mission tracked and paid once, pay-at-Rook once, data file wiring); `tests/test_story_save.py` (cloud save keeps the tracked side mission and the flag); `tests/flight-combat-browser.cjs` part D now grants the coordinates first; `tests/flight-map-browser.py` (Python Playwright: route-only markers and Map app, full mode, Rook's line, board, a job in the Mission Log).
- **Not verified:** the whole Awakening chain 001–012 played to the end in a browser before Rook's board (the M012 hand-in was checked in Node); a full side-mission flight with combat in the browser (the delivery is covered in Node); real graphics hardware.

### 20 Nov 2026 (newest): THE UNDERGROUND — ONE TUNNEL NETWORK JOINING THE THREE STAIR DOORS (branch `claude/tunnel-network`, build `2026-11-20-tunnel-network-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner: "all three entrances connect into one network. add the fighting pit but make it so that npc can walk to every other area without having to walk through the fighting pit area. add the tea room with the sleeping room connected to the back of it. the mahjong parlour. the food stall. maybe small alcoves along the way with seats, lights, etc."

- **What it is (`void-runner/ncz-underground.js`, `VoidUnderground`; wiring `ncz-underground-integration.js`):** a **quarter of the NeoChinatown street of its own**, id `underground` (walkingLocation id `neochinatown`, area `street`, quarter `underground`), a shared public place like the streets (not a mission location). Built from the existing underground modules (modules.js) as ONE module location laid out by `modules.js layout()` with a fixed grid offset (`OFF` [0.27, -0.5]: the Old Town and Far South checkpoint doors stand exactly at their stair doors; the Avenue's is 1.1 m short, its exit door's dark vestibule fills the gap), in TRUE world metres, the floor 3 m below the street (y -3, the stair landings' depth). The trunk runs north-south at x -3.7..-1.7 (west of every street rectangle above, so no point is ever in two areas), z +5.5 to -186.5; 74 modules. In that quarter `neochinatown.js` builds only the network (no city, crowd, weather, taxis); Reverie / Lace / made characters start there as in any quarter (street pair).
- **Place ids (stable; the editor's place list, places.js parent `underground`, npc-areas.js area `underground`):** `under-checkpoint-avenue`, `under-avenue-passage`, `under-trunk-north`, `under-alcove-1` (vending machine), `under-food-stall`, `under-alcove-2` (wall shrine), `under-mahjong`, `under-checkpoint-oldtown`, `under-oldtown-passage`, `under-trunk-south`, `under-alcove-3` (old TV), `under-tea-room`, `under-sleeping-room`, `under-alcove-4` (drinks cooler), `under-fight-pit-passage`, `under-fight-pit`, `under-alcove-5` (valve wheel), `under-alcove-6` (lantern), `under-checkpoint-farsouth`, `under-farsouth-passage`. Module ids inside: trunk junctions `j1`..`j13`, tubes `t1`.., checkpoints `ck-av` / `ck-ot` / `ck-fs`, rooms `food`, `mj`, `tea`, `flop`, `pp` (pit passage), `pit`, alcoves `al1`..`al6`.
- **Door links:** `under-avenue` stair door (17.37, -3, 10.53) <-> `ck-av.e2` (16.27, 10.5); `under-oldtown` (6.27, -73) <-> `ck-ot.e2` (6.27, -73.5); `under-farsouth` (28.27, -187.5) <-> `ck-fs.e2` (28.27, -187.5). On the stair landing: "GO DOWN INTO THE TUNNELS" (a fade; inside the checkpoint facing west). At each checkpoint's outer door: "GO UP THE STAIRS TO THE STREET" (the door opens, a fade, on that stairwell's landing facing up). `VoidUnderground.arrive(id)` / `leave(id)` give the spawn points. The tea room's front door (`tea.s1`) opens on the trunk; its hidden back door (`tea.n1`) is the sleeping room's front door (`flop.s1`); the sleeping room has no other exit. The fight pit (`pit.e1`) is the dead end of its own passage off junction `j10`.
- **The fight pit is never on the way:** it has one way in; `tests/underground.test.cjs` checks every pair of other places (the walk graph, and real NPC routes over the walkable ground) and the mission arrow's routes. One file holds the data, rules and the build: `void-runner/ncz-backalleys.js` (`VoidBackAlleys`); the game wiring is `void-runner/ncz-backalleys-integration.js`.

- **Where (29 places, ids = place ids in the editor):** Avenue `avenue-north-alley` / `-yard` / `-bay` (U behind doors 23-45 north side), `avenue-southwest-*` (behind 16-38 south), `avenue-southeast-*` (behind 86-114 south); Old Town `oldtown-null-alley` / `-bay` / `-yard` (round NULL's west side; NULL stays closed), `oldtown-north-alley` / `-yard` (Main Street east at z -50..-45 to the District alley at x 109-114), `oldtown-terrace-lane` / `-alley` / `-east-lane` / `-south-alley` / `-yard` / `-bay` (loop round the terrace; the terrace's own collision walls reach z -119.9, so the south alley runs at z -125..-120); Far South `far-south-east-alley` / `-alley-south` / `-lane` (into Milked's side street) / `-yard` / `-yard-south` / `-bay`, `far-south-west-alley` / `-bay` / `-yard` (behind 176-190). Alleys 4-5 m wide (mouths 4.1-4.8 m where doors sit close), bays about 9 x 14 m, yards 12 x 12 (Old Town north yard 12 x 10, Avenue south-east yard 12 x 11, Far South east bay only 7.5 m deep: no more room).
- **How:** the pieces are added to `neochinatown.js` streets (so the city blocks and plot buildings are carved round them; the plots show back facades) and to each quarter's streets (`ncz-quarters.js`: the mission arrow routes in, `quarterAt` finds them). The shopfront row is cut at each mouth (panels either side; no sign / wall advert / kiosk in front; random numbers run as before), the District alley's walls open where the two Old Town alleys join it. Since the city blocks are cut differently, the plot buildings' random looks elsewhere in the city changed (seeded sequence). Dressing (one merged mesh per material, ~21 per quarter): wet concrete with puddles, grime, pipes, downpipes, vents, posters, cables (Medium+), sodium / dim coloured wall lamps with additive pools and washes (no real lights), dumpsters, crates, pallets, drums, generators, a loading dock with a roller shutter in every bay. Furniture is solid for the player and an obstacle for NPCs.
- **Fire escapes (18):** behind apartment doors whose wall straight behind is an alley (avenue-n-23/30/37/44, avenue-s-16/23/30/37/86/93/100/107/114, main-w-33 (in the Avenue's south-west yard), main-e-82/68, main-w-176/183/190); `escapeRule()` and a test keep the list honest. Landing 3.2 m up, a second level and stairs (Medium+), a drop ladder hanging 1.55 m off the ground. **Jump** at its foot facing the wall: grab and climb (1.7 s); on the landing jump or walk off the ladder end to climb down. The window prompt shows only if a taken mission uses that door; it opens only when the apartment is available (its from-step reached): in through the window to that hallway's FAR end (x +9.55, facing back down the corridor). No hack option; a hallway door locked until hacked does not keep the window shut. The hallway's own window (east end) gets "CLIMB OUT OF THE WINDOW" (only for a building with a fire escape) -> the foot of the fire escape.

### 20 Nov 2026: CROSSWALKS IN NEOCHINATOWN (branch `claude/crosswalks`, build `2026-11-20-crosswalks-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner: "Another thing NeoChinaTown needs are crosswalks". One file holds where they are and the rules: `void-runner/crosswalks.js` (`VoidCrosswalks`, pure part tested in Node).

- **Where (15):** Old Town Main Street at the North Gate (z -29.5), the District alley / NULL (-58.5), mid-block (-88), the access-road corner by the South Gate (-116.5); across the access road's mouth (x 52.5) and in front of Port Customs (x 39.2); the Avenue at the Function Gate (x 14), both sides of the North Gate junction (50.5, 71.5), mid-block (107), the East Gate (140); Far South at the South Gate (-133), north of Milked's side street (-161), south of its service alley (-199), the Far South Gate (-213.5). Placed clear of the kerb's ad screens, kiosks and taxi poles. No stretch of Main Street or the Avenue goes more than 40 m without one.
- **Look:** worn white zebra bars and a stop line before each (right-hand traffic), painted on a canvas texture with chipped edges, tyre scuffs, grime and cracks, lit PBR paint with the road's normal map (wet sheen) and only a faint self-light (readable, not glowing). Junctions and gates also get a dark steel signal post on each kerb (push-button box, head with a dim hand and a lit walking figure: unlit emissive, no real light). The yellow centre dashes stop at the crossings. **Draw calls:** one merged mesh of paint per quarter, one of posts, one of icons (+3 per quarter whatever the number of crossings; Low: paint only, +1).
- **Used:** street walkers (`ncz-nightlife.js`): a lone sober walker reaching a crosswalk crosses with `crossShare` 0.3 (looks for a moving cab first, waits up to 8 s), then not again for 30 s; nobody crosses anywhere else. Mission characters leading / following (`npc-escort.js walkRoute`, a two-line change: a crosswalk cell costs 1.2 instead of the road's 8) cross on the stripes. The mission arrow (`guide-route.js plan`) inserts the near and far kerb of the best crossing when a leg goes from one pavement to the other (hysteresis: the crossing in use wins ties). Road taxis (`road-taxi-rules.js stepWorld`, new 4th argument `peds`) slow and stop about a metre short of a crossing ahead with anyone on it: the player on foot or anyone marked with `VoidCrosswalks.mark` (crossing walkers, moving mission characters); somebody waiting on the pavement does not stop traffic.
- **Placement:** crosswalks are road, so `npc-areas.js` never places anyone on one (unchanged rule); the signal posts are added to its obstacles (admin inventory regenerated); the walkers' stand / stop spots keep 1.8 m clear of every crossing's kerb.
- **Tests:** `tests/crosswalks.test.cjs`; `tests/guide-route.test.cjs` expectations now include the alley crossing. **Checked in a browser** (bundled Chromium, software GL, Medium): junction and mid-block crossings in Old Town, the Avenue and Far South at street level, a walker crossing at Old Town's mid-block crossing, both cabs stopping short of it with the player standing on it outside their lane, the signal icon close up; draw calls at the alley junction 565 with the crossings, 563 without (3 meshes per quarter; the paint and posts are left out of the glow pass). Screenshots were in the session's scratchpad (not in the repo). **Not verified:** real GPU / ROG Ally frame rate, VR. Reverie and Lace never use the alley crossing (its west kerb is NULL's door, a content rule), they take the next one.

Reproduced in a browser with every frame recorded. Full causes and fixes: [`MISSION_EDITOR.md`](MISSION_EDITOR.md), "Fix (19 Nov 2026): a giver who leads the way".

- **Fixes:** her mission holds her (waits-at spot, else where she stood, after a follow step its spot) until it is done (`mission-content.js holdOf`, `live-content.js plans()` `holdAt` / `why:'mission'` / `from`); held for the whole conversation (her lines); appears where the step started; never popped out while seen (main's `holdCheck` / `p.going`, merged); pavement routes with straight road crossings (`npc-escort.js walkRoute`).
- **Tests:** `tests/follow-giver.test.cjs`, browser `tests/follow-giver-browser.cjs` (passes for Reverie Avenue / Old Town, Lace, the cat, a made character); source-text tests updated. No save shape change. **Not verified:** real hardware, VR, a shift change mid-mission in the live game.

The owner: "Admin will also sell a pistol, the base pistol. You can walk around freely in NeoChinatown. But you can't fire it there. Add a small Green 'Friendly' indicator in the top left ... Orange in 'Neutral' areas ... if you fire it you will be attacked ... red 'Hostile' area the enemy will try to kill you if you're seen and you can shoot back"; then "I'll let you know which areas are which", "let me change a room or area from friendly to hostile in the mission timeline", and a worry about players drawing by accident. Editor how-to: [`MISSION_EDITOR.md`](MISSION_EDITOR.md), "Twenty-third pass".

- **The pistol:** Admin's counter (a new campaign: talking to Admin opens "Admin's stock"; the scrambler shows there once Admin has introduced it) sells PISTOL (`base-pistol`) for Combat Balance `pistolPrice` (350), once. Saved in `progression.personal.weapons` (client `progression.js buyWeapon`/`restore`, server `void_runner_progression.py WEAPONS`); the drawn gun is `personal.weapon`. Draw / holster with the existing 1 / H keys and the weapon wheel (`mouse-control.js`); the existing first-person pistol model, a muzzle flash and a procedural bang (no laser sound in the city).
- **The chip** (top left, under the title and the multiplayer chip): ◆ FRIENDLY green "WEAPONS LOCKED", ▲ NEUTRAL orange "FIRING MAKES THEM ATTACK", ✖ HOSTILE red "ENEMIES ATTACK ON SIGHT"; only while walking in NeoChinatown (stations and the Vesper range keep the old behaviour).
- **Rules (decided):** in Friendly the gun may still be drawn and holstered (looking at it is harmless; the owner asked that drawing never matters), only the trigger is refused ("Weapons are locked here."). Drawing / holstering never provokes anyone and never fails a step. Neutral: a SHOT provokes the armed people of that location / area for `guardHostileTime` (45 s; leaving calms them at once) and walkers within `civilianFleeRadius` turn and walk away; the **safety catch** (`neutralSafety`, on) makes the first press warn and a second within `safetyWindow` (2 s) fire; holstering re-arms it. Hostile: armed people see the player within `detectRange` (22 m, 60% crouching) inside `detectCone` (110°, all round once alerted), with no wall or closed door between, and shoot (Skirmish AI). No PvP anywhere. Taken down: TAKEN DOWN card, GET UP outside the location's street door (or in the District hall), health 100; the mission is retried or fails (abandoned: back to its giver) per its setting. Health comes back slowly after 8 s without a hit.
- **Who decides the zones:** everything defaults to FRIENDLY. Developer Tools > **Zones** (new tab): every NeoChinatown area and every location type; saved in the draft (`zones`), published as `content.zones`. Per mission, "Zones and armed people" (rooms, location, city areas while it runs, armed people: security guard / mercenary / enforcer, guard or patrol, onDeath). The **Zone block / "Change zone" step** changes a room, location or city area from that point until a later step or the mission's end, optionally bringing in the armed people placed there. All numbers are in Combat Balance > "On foot". Nothing was set to Neutral or Hostile in the shipped data.
- **One combat balance for on foot** (the owner: "if something is changed in ground combat like how much damage guns do also change it in the Skirmish FPS mode"): the campaign and Skirmish FPS both read `zones.js` `gun()` / `skirmish()` from Combat Balance > On foot: pistol damage, aimed bonus (34 → 42), time between shots, spread, recoil, player health, the enemies' health, damage, accuracy, reaction, speed, detection range and cone; magazine (10), reserve and reload time stay `progression.js tuning.ground` (the save server checks the magazine). Skirmish difficulty only scales the enemies' Normal values. Changes to Skirmish from this: magazine 12 → 10, reload 1.35 → 1.1 s, time between shots .27 → .25 s, raiders 54 / 70 → 60 / 81 health, they spot the player within 26 m in a 110° cone (was 29 m all round; they start facing the hangar), shots have spread and recoil. Test: `tests/zones.test.cjs` ("ONE combat balance"), browser `tests/skirmish-balance-browser.cjs`.
- **Files:** `zones.js` (pure rules and checks), `zone-integration.js` (game), `zone-editor.js` (editor), small hooks in `faction-scenes.js`, `progression.js`, `mouse-control.js`, `mission-content.js`, `live-content.js` (`zoneStep`), `npc-device.js` (spectacle `flee`), `mission-blocks*.js`, `mission-editor.js`, `mission-givers.js`, `dev-tools.js`, `balance-data.js`; server `void_runner_live_content.py` (`zone_table_problems`, `combat_problems`, `zone_step_problems`), `void_runner_drafts.py` (workspace `zones`), `void_runner_progression.py`; admin inventory `zones`.
- **Tests:** `tests/zones.test.cjs`, `tests/test_zones.py`, browser `tests/zones-browser.cjs` (Admin sells the pistol; Friendly blocks the trigger; a Neutral hallway: the first press warns, the second fires and the guard attacks; a Friendly office turned Hostile by a "Change zone" step: red chip, the mercenary appears, sees and shoots, and is shot down; taken down and GET UP outside).
- **Not done / not verified:** the armed people are the Skirmish raider's procedural body in three outfits (no Meshy figures or animation clips yet; legs swing); no cover-taking or reloading enemies; no civilians inside locations; street areas have no guards of their own (a Neutral street only makes walkers flee); hitting a civilian is not modelled; no ammunition for sale (the pistol has its 10 + 48 rounds, refilled on respawn); real hardware / ROG Ally, VR, two real players. Admin's shop is shared with the flight-combat work (`claude/flight-combat`): when both merge, keep both item sets in `adminStore`.

### 19 Nov 2026 (newest): THE BLACKROCK CULT ROOM — A HACKED CAMERA FEED, RECORDED TO THE V1 GALLERY (branch `claude/blackrock-cult`, build `2026-11-19-blackrock-cult-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

- **Flow:** a "Hack the access point" step with a **Camera feed** switches the screen to the cult room's security camera (top corner, looking down, the V1 Camera App look with REC; wheel zoom); the player's world is paused (game.js `update` not run) and their keys do nothing; the scene plays and **records itself** (MediaRecorder, WebM, burn-in), the video is saved in the **V1 Gallery** (new: videos, 5% each, 48 MB cap, this browser only, a player view), the view returns to the player; **the step completes when the recording is saved** (event `feed`).
- **The room** (its own Babylon scene, built only for the feed): 14 m square, 7 m high, board-formed concrete, ceiling services, the owner's owl as a weathered stencil (`art/blackrock/owl-stencil.webp`, owner-supplied art), a black stone altar with candles and a box with the paper card, a sliding door with a stub corridor behind it.

- **The scene is an editable timeline** (`mission.feeds[i].scene`, `cult-room.js`): the owner's choreography with every line slot EMPTY (no invented words; empty = silence, nothing shown) and IMPORT VOICE FILE per line; beats, pauses, speeds, turns, walks, animations, the circle; PREVIEW; client and server checks. Listed under **Other** in Add a location (camera feed only).
- **Tests:** `tests/cult-room.test.cjs`, `tests/test_cult_room.py`, browser `tests/cult-room-browser.cjs` (hack -> feed -> recording saved and played in the Gallery -> back to the player).

Low: fewer LEDs, slower chase. The plate's backing grew from 0.98 x 0.53 to 1.06 x 0.61 m to hold the strip. The two far signs' cyan outline is now dark amber (the LEDs are the frame).

Data: `void-runner/npc-areas.js` (the game's own street, pavement and room geometry; furniture and ground height read from the built scenes by `tests/npc-obstacles-browser.cjs`, plus the `layout()` collision solids).
- **Never (the owner's content rule):** Milked (building, side street, service alley, the pavement at their mouths), NULL (club and the pavement at its door), the District bar, VR play area and arcade room (left out to be safe), the Sleeping Pod room (the players' own pods), the smoking terrace, the Corporate District, the Temple. Also blocked: roads (taxi lanes), gate insides, the meeting room, the security vestibule, behind the shop counter, walls and furniture. Checked in the editor, by `mission-content.js` / `npc-paths.js` (play, publish), by the server (`void_runner_live_content.py area_check`, same decisions: a parity test) and in the editor's server copy of the draft (`forbidden_spot_problems`, `void_runner_drafts.py`).
- **Editor:** an area list on the map (plain names); each area drawn with its walkable ground, roads, buildings / rooms, gates, furniture, posts and labels; a click on blocked ground snaps to the nearest allowed spot (8 m at most) or shows the plain reason (a forbidden place is never snapped out of). A route stays in one area (the path system has no NPC gate crossing): a point in another area is refused with the reason; use a time block per area. PLAY THIS DRAFT / JUMP THERE open the test session in the spot's area. The ChatGPT prompt lists every area with its rectangles, the never-used places and named places in every area.
- **Game:** `neochinatown.js` starts the pair in every street quarter and inside the District; `ncz-street-pair.js` loads and moves her (and the cat) only in the area where her plan puts her (her usual routine stays on the Avenue). A spot or time block in another area: she is there from the start of her shift / that block (no walk across town); a new spot she cannot walk to straight is a re-placement, not a walk through a wall. The cat follows its own plan, else whichever of them is out, wherever she is.
- **Follow / lead / cab steps (#414):** `npc-escort.js placedArea` (where she waits for the mission, else her all-day placement, else The Avenue); the step's spot must be in that area and cab stops in its quarter (`stops(id, area)`, greyed with the reason; admin inventory `npcEscort.npcs[id].stopsByArea`; server `placed_area` / `escort_problems(o, inv, area)`); away from the Avenue she walks straight or round one corner (`route`) and calls the cab from beside the nearest taxi pole (`kerb`).
- **Tests:** `tests/npc-areas.test.cjs`, `tests/test_npc_areas.py`, browser `tests/npc-areas-browser.cjs`; older npc and escort tests updated for the wider ground.

### 19 Nov 2026: MISSION EDITOR BUG FIX — PLAY STRAIGHT AFTER TYPING (branch `claude/play-stale-fix`, build `2026-11-19-play-fix-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)
### 19 Nov 2026: FOLLOW / LEAD / SHARED-CAB MISSION STEPS (branch `claude/follow-steps`, build `2026-11-19-follow-steps-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged; built on `claude/npc-behaviour`)

Queue item 3, the "Follow / lead steps" part (approved 18 Nov 2026; the owner asked for it next). Full how-to: [`MISSION_EDITOR.md`](MISSION_EDITOR.md), "Fifteenth pass".

- **Four new step kinds** in the Mission Editor's step list and the "Make a mission" wizard, for anyone who can walk with the player (npc-paths.js `MOVABLE`: Reverie, Lace, the street cat; everyone else greyed with the reason, and more plug in as their walking code follows a plan): **Follow them** (to a spot picked on the map; waits when the player is more than N m behind, default 8; resumes when they catch up; beyond M m, default 40, the owner's choice: stop and wait, or the mission fails and goes back to the giver), **They follow you** (about 2 m behind on the player's own trail, walking or running at the player's pace, put behind the player only when 20 m+ away and off-screen; the cat at its own pace), **They call a cab** (she walks to the kerb and calls a road taxi to the chosen stop, gets in, the player boards with BOARD TAXI, both get out there), **You call a cab, they ride along** (she follows; gets into the player's cab to the chosen stop; both get out). Done when both arrive / both are out.
- **Where:** spots on her own pavement (the Avenue's north pavement); cab stops only The Avenue's three (The Avenue, Function Gate, East Gate): the street pair and the cat are built only in the Avenue quarter, so Old Town, The Port, Far South, the Corporate District and the Temple are greyed with the reason (Reverie / Lace city only). The cat does not ride cabs. Fares as normal (developer account free).
- **Her shift (street clock, `streetTime()`):** a step with Reverie or Lace starts only while she is out (a draft test simulates a time she is out); if her shift ends mid-step she finishes the step, then leaves. She is never brought out off-shift, so she is never in two places (Milked Studio 1 shows the off-shift one).

Server: `void_runner_live_content.py escort_problems` (admin inventory `npcEscort`), `escort_shape_problems` (`void_runner_drafts.py`).
- **Multiplayer:** the street is not shared: only the mission player's NPC follows them; other players see her as usual.
- **Rules kept:** Reverie / Lace only on the Avenue, only their casual clips (walk, run, idle, sit); voices untouched; the Sleeping Pod and the music player untouched; no new save fields beyond the one story flag.
- **Tests:** `tests/npc-escort.test.cjs` (8: wait / resume / lost-pause / lost-fail, completion, trail following and catch-up, the cat's pace, cab boarding and the cab leaving, shared-cab stop rules, content checks, export, wizard, inventory, the save flag), `tests/test_npc_escort.py` (4), browser `tests/npc-escort-browser.cjs` (the editor panels; then a wizard-made draft given by Reverie: follow her to a spot with a stop-and-wait, she follows the player to a spot, she calls a cab and both ride and get out, the player calls a cab and she rides along; `PART=editor`).

### 19 Nov 2026: MISSION EDITOR BUG FIX — PLAY STRAIGHT AFTER TYPING (branch `claude/play-stale-fix`, build `2026-11-19-play-fix-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

Reported: PLAY THIS DRAFT pressed straight after typing in a step said "step 1 needs what the player must do" although the saved draft was complete (the wizard's PLAY IT NOW worked).

- **Cause:** `mission-editor.js play()` found the mission in `built` (the trees drawn before the typing); typing saves the draft and only marks `built` stale, so the play check read the old step. The PLAY button was also greyed until Guide me's 350 ms pause, and a status line that was not on screen yet made `setStatus` redraw the whole editor on the first keystroke (the typing focus was lost after one character).
- **Fix:** one helper, `fresh()` (the field being typed in hands over its value, the trees are rebuilt from the saved draft, a waiting Guide me pass runs now), called first by `play` (PLAY THIS DRAFT, PLAY THIS DRAFT FROM HERE, Test my mission / PLAY IT NOW via `shared.playTree`) and `jumpTo` (JUMP THERE, SEE IT ON THE STREET, NPC draft plays); PUBLISH and ASK THE SERVER TO CHECK refuse and re-check if the draft changed since the check screen was drawn; `syncPlay` greys / un-greys the PLAY buttons on every keystroke (same `treeBlockers` rule); `setStatus` adds its line in place instead of redrawing.
- **Tests:** `tests/play-stale.test.cjs` (4), browser `tests/play-stale-browser.cjs` (empty step, OPEN TO FIX, type and press PLAY THIS DRAFT at once: it starts with the typed words; PLAY FROM HERE and Test my mission after an edit; passes, bundled Chromium, software GL; on the old code it fails with the reported message).
- **Not verified:** publishing with the server switched on; real hardware.

Queue item 1 (approved 18 Nov 2026), plus the owner's "add the cat as an npc and giver". Full how-to: [`MISSION_EDITOR.md`](MISSION_EDITOR.md), "Twelfth pass".

- **Describe it to ChatGPT** (NPCs tab, every NPC the editor can move: Reverie, Lace, the street cat): COPY CHATGPT PROMPT copies a self-contained prompt (the recipe schema, that NPC's named places with coordinates, the pavement bounds and obstacles, its allowed actions from its real clip list after the content rules, the limits, two examples); the owner adds what he wants in words. **Paste behaviour** box: the JSON is found in the answer (```json fences and chatter tolerated), read with `JSON.parse` only, checked field by field (unknown keys, places off the pavement, actions the NPC may not do, too many points / blocks, bad or overlapping times; anything that looks like code is refused outright) with plain fix-it messages, previewed in words and on the map (per time block), tried with PLAY IT (a draft session, not saved), then USE THIS BEHAVIOUR saves it into the same placement the route editor makes (`content.npcs[id]`; UNDO works). Nothing pasted is ever run: `void-runner/npc-behaviour.js` (pure, Node-tested) has no eval / Function / innerHTML.
- **Daily schedule** (same tab): time blocks on the **in-game clock** (`game-clock.js`; an in-game day is 2 hours of play), e.g. 08:00–12:00 walk a route, 12:00–13:00 sit on the ledge, 20:00–02:00 stay at a spot; a 24-hour timeline, a table (times, what she does, name, EDIT ON THE MAP, remove), + ADD A TIME BLOCK, "Try a time" slider and PLAY THIS DRAFT AT THIS TIME (the test session's clock set to that time). Outside every block the all-day placement applies. Reverie and Lace still keep their 12-hour UTC street shift: the schedule only says what she does while she is out. Stored as `content.npcs[id].schedule [{from:'HH:MM', to, name?, mode:'stay'|'path'|'original', ...}]`; at most 8 blocks, at least 15 minutes each, no overlaps, past midnight allowed. Checked by `npc-paths.js scheduleProblems` (client, PLAY / PUBLISH) and `void_runner_live_content.py schedule_problems` (server); the editor's server copy checks the shape (`void_runner_drafts.py`, `schedule_shape_problems`). Game: `live-content.js plans()` passes the clock's minute of the day to `npc-paths.js planNow` (`blockAt`), so she changes block as the in-game hour passes.

(text only, no voice, no lore); while petted it comes to the player and sits looking up. Works with "When walked near" and "Giver waits at" (it sits / looks / potters there).
- **Rules kept:** Reverie / Lace casual clips only, city only (the Avenue's north pavement); voices untouched; the Sleeping Pod and music player untouched; no save fields.

### 18 Nov 2026: SITE SLIMMING — SMALLER PUBLISHED SITE, NOTHING VISIBLE OR AUDIBLE CHANGED (branch `claude/site-slim`, build `2026-11-18-site-slim-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner approved shrinking the GitHub Pages site with no visible or audible change. Published tracked files: 650.5 MB (origin/main) -> 517.6 MB (tools/assets/build-pages.cjs with no licensed files: 2,093 files, 517.6 MB; the licensed models add what they did before) MB.

- **Not shipped any more (still in the repository):** `tools/assets/build-pages.cjs` `UNSHIPPED` (about 30 MB): the `attached_assets` PNG originals whose `.webp` twins the pages use (cityscape, cityscape fade, sleeping-pod background art, `top.png`, `Iris 01.png`, `Iris 01(1).png`), `cyber-hoodie.jpg` (a 49-byte stub), root `01_bg_near.png` / `01_bg_far.png` (Blackout loads its own copies), Blackout's `Neuroforge.png` and `platform_rail.png`, `void-runner/art/vesper-ridges.webp`, and `iris-service/` (the Render chat service's source). Each was checked unreferenced (case-insensitive, URL-encoded, built-up paths, service worker, sitemap); `tests/build-pages.test.cjs` keeps it so.
- **PNG re-saves (1.8 MB):** 35 PNGs re-compressed losslessly (oxipng level 6, every chunk kept, decoded pixels compared identical). 24 shipped VOID//RUNNER PNGs (`void-runner/art/` paintings, `meridian-panels-v1.png`) carry C2PA content credentials (`caBX`), which a re-save drops: those were left byte-for-byte alone. The Sleeping Pod room and music player files were not touched.
- **Blackout Protocol textures as lossless WebP (14.7 MB):** 75 PNGs -> `.webp` (VP8L, `exact`, decoded RGBA compared identical); `engine.js` and the shop / game-over / leaderboard / complete pages load the `.webp`; the PNGs (with their C2PA `caBX` credentials, which the WebP copies do not carry) stay in the repository and in `static/`, and are not published (`BLACKOUT_WEBP`). `make_seamless.js` (an offline tool) still reads the PNGs.
- **VOID//RUNNER GLBs, lossless meshopt (66.2 MB):** 186 GLBs re-written with gltf-transform (meshoptimizer 1.3.0 encoder, `reorder` + `EXT_meshopt_compression` with no filters and no quantisation; node transforms copied back exactly). Each checked against its original: textures byte-identical; nodes (names, order, hierarchy, exact TRS), skins (joint order, inverse bind matrices), materials, morph targets, animation names and every key (times and values bit-identical), and every triangle with all its vertex attributes (as a multiset) identical. gltfpack was **not** used: even with `-noq` it quantises skin weights to 8 bits, and `-cc` rounds animation keys. Kept as they were: `vr/models/dolphin.glb` (its Node test loads it without a browser decoder), the `kit/` pieces (external textures), the tiny meridian / gear files that did not shrink, six files whose re-write differed in a raw-JSON detail (chair, wall-cables x2, wall-panel, rib, desk-medium) and `mara-poc.glb` (dev animation lab loads it without the asset pipeline). Loader: `asset-pipeline.js validate()` accepts `EXT_meshopt_compression` (still refuses Draco / KHR_meshopt); `useMeshoptLocally()` points `BABYLON.MeshoptCompression` at `void-runner/vendor/meshopt_decoder-1.3.0.js` (MIT, `vendor/LICENSE-meshoptimizer.md`) before any model loads; `cache.js` warms it into the offline cache. Tests and tools that read GLB data decode meshopt views (`tests/glb-data.cjs`; `tools/assets/glb-lib.cjs` after `await ready`). `extract-hair-parts.cjs --check` now reports the (compressed) hair parts as changed bytes; the bind data it computes from the compressed bodies is identical apart from the hair files' byte counts. `tests/meshopt-models.test.cjs` (new).
- **Book PDF (20 MB):** `attached_assets/The Darknet District text4.pdf` (same name, 53 pages, text layer and docinfo kept): its 26 page images were quality-100 JPEGs; re-encoded at quality 88 (4:2:0 as before, optimised, progressive), same 1375 px. Rendered at 150 dpi the pages measure 44-48 dB PSNR against the original; text pages are pixel-identical. Crops were compared by eye.
- **Verified in a browser** (bundled Chromium, software GL, Medium): before/after screenshots of the character creator, three bodies with a fitted hairstyle and all four gear pieces, the District interior, the street crowd, Reverie and Lace on the Avenue, Milked, VR Kai and Zane; Blackout Protocol title, all six levels, shop, game over, leaderboard, complete. Same model files requested, same console errors before and after (only the pre-existing `reading 'update'` page error and the 404s for licensed `.vrpak` files that are not in a local checkout). Babylon 8.26 itself loaded all 187 recompressed GLBs next to their originals (NullEngine) with identical vertex and index counts, position and skin-weight sums, bone order, materials, textures, morph targets and animation keys, and identical bone poses at three frames of four clips for the 93 crowd / VR / Milked models. Hair refits and gear on three bodies gave identical vertex sums before and after. Screenshots and pairs: the session scratchpad `slim/` (not in the repository).
- **Not verified:** real GPU / VR headset; High and Low tiers; the live Pages deploy (sealed licensed models are unchanged and not compressed).

### 18 Nov 2026: DEVELOPER TOOLS — HELP TEXT ON EVERY SETTING, "GUIDE ME", "GIVER WAITS AT" ON A MAP, AND A NEW "NPCs" TAB (branch `claude/editor-npc-tools`, build `2026-11-18-npc-tools-1`, pushed, NO PR, not merged)

The owner asked for short descriptions on the editor's settings, a map to place givers on, a preview, and "another tab inside developer tools for editing NPCs, adding givers, a preview, NPC paths and actions"; then, while it was being built: move NPCs on the map and save them at a spot, change their routes or make them stay, add animations ("any animations in the game ... that can use them"), import new models (hard polygon cap), short help in the NPC editor too, "highlight the next box that needs an input", and make it for someone who has never done it. Full how-to: [`MISSION_EDITOR.md`](MISSION_EDITOR.md), "Eighth pass".

- **Help text** (Mission Editor): one or two plain lines under every setting; every dropdown (Starts, Trigger, step kind, How it is said, unlock kind) shows what the chosen option does and a "?" that lists them all. Location says plainly that it is a Mission Log label and moves nobody.
- **Guide me** (`void-runner/editor-guide.js`, on by default, a switch remembered per browser): the next required box pulses with "Next: ..." (the same list as the play / publish check: `mission-content.js treeNeeds`, of which `treeBlockers` is now the plain-words copy); when nothing is left, "All required fields filled" and PLAY THIS DRAFT is highlighted. Focus moves only after "+ NEW ...". Reduced motion: no pulse.
- **Giver waits at** (Reverie, Lace): in a mission they give, click the map (The Avenue, top-down, `void-runner/npc-map.js`, drawn from the street's own data) to place the spot, drag it, turn the facing handle, face N/E/S/W, JUMP THERE (a throwaway session a few steps from it). While that mission waits to be offered (and she is out) she walks there and does her standing routine (idle, look, V1; sits if the spot is at a ledge); once accepted or done she walks back. Only the Avenue's north pavement between the gates, clear of the taxi posts (crossing the road would put her in front of the taxis). `mission.waitAt {x,z,yaw}`; `mission-content.js waitingAt`; `live-content.js plans()` sets `VoidStreetPair.planFor`.
- **NPCs tab** (`void-runner/npc-editor.js`, loaded on demand with the Mission Editor and sharing its draft): a "What do you want to do?" start card with six big choices; every NPC the game knows (72: givers, residents, earlier characters, the street crowd's figures, imported models), with look (model file), where, voice (READ ONLY), a 3D preview with their real clips (`void-runner/npc-preview.js`, its own small engine; licensed files say they are not in the public files), "Give them a mission" (a new draft mission given by them, no lines written), placement for the movable ones (Reverie, Lace: their usual thing / keep her at one spot / make her walk a route, actions from her own clip list), Add animation (every clip in the game, indexed from the real files: `tools/npc-clip-index.cjs` -> `void-runner/npc-clip-index.json`; usable ones are those her rig takes through the animation library's runtime retarget, rest pose within 25 degrees, and that pass her content rule), Request animation (an exported list; licensed No AI models marked local retarget only), Lines & voice (the old "All lines for an NPC", moved here, with search, counts and OPEN THIS MISSION), Import model (local test only, IndexedDB; hard cap 50,000 triangles and 15 MB, amber over 20,000 or textures over 2048 px; placed and played in a test session; ADD TO THE GAME downloads the file and a package JSON with licence answers for Claude/Codex). Advanced switch shows internal ids.
- **Content and server:** `content.npcs {id: {mode:'stay'|'path'|'original', ..., anims}}` and `mission.waitAt`, checked by `mission-content.js` (through `void-runner/npc-paths.js`, pure) and the server (`void_runner_live_content.py npc_problems`, `wait_at_problems`, `import_package_problems`, from the admin inventory's new `npcPaths`). The editor's draft, server copy, export, PLAY THIS DRAFT and PUBLISH carry them. No save fields.

Milked Studio 1 (#395) is untouched; no studio or club placements or clips are offered for Reverie or Lace.

Voices are never changed. The Sleeping Pod and the music player are untouched.
